### J-PILOT: Cross-app journeys and physical pilot acceptance (MVP-49/50, pilot-acceptance-runbook.md) and product decisions MVP-01..07

Software: every cross-app journey J01-J05 (J02 is loop B) has a browser journey spec that drives rendered controls with the development profile. These are test/browser/journey-j01-poster-calendar, journey-b-voice-note-actions, journey-j03-document-analysis, journey-j04-schedule-travel and journey-j05-web-research-note. A flag-off production spec (journey-core-loops.production.spec.ts) covers J01, J03, J04 and J05 in the web bundle; it covers loops A, C, D and F, but none of J01-J05, with the Android bridge stubbed. Provisioning and update tooling exists and is host-tested (scripts/provision-unit.mjs, scripts/pilot-update.mjs, test/pilot-units.test.mjs). It admits only distributable releases, and android/release-signer.json is still "unset", so no release can be admitted.

What keeps software below about 70%:
- The flag-off path is only tested where it fails closed. J03 and J05 are tested with no agent; nothing exercises a connected resident agent answering and the explicit save. J02 is never run together on the Android path.
- J04 has no production Maps provider (A-23), so a production user gets an "unavailable" state.
- No composite cross-app instrumented test exists on the Android side. Each journey is evidenced only by per-component instrumented classes.
- The voice entrypoint latency marks are test.todo.
- Every product decision MVP-01..06 is open: A-01/03/04/05/06/09/10/11-20/22-26 all await the owner.

Acceptance: class E evidence exists for most closable J-steps. It was recorded at round 7/8 on developer-override APKs with an unpackaged runtime, which is not distributable-build evidence. J05-2 is still EMULATOR. There is no distributable APK at pin 13c9857598. There is no I evidence (no AOSP image or Cuttlefish boot), no R evidence (Gmail OAuth 401, no Maps provider, no real agent answer for J05-9), and no D evidence. The emulator provisioning rehearsal has never been recorded (there is no test-results/pilot-units). All four runbook unit rows and all upgrade-from-mock gates are "not run". MVP-50 is 0% on the evidence side.

Stale or wrong claims found:

- docs/mvp-remaining-work-2026-10-09.md and .json, MVP-49 'current' has stale counts. It says 'J01 6 of 9, J02 (loop B) 17 of 23, J03 8 of 9, J04 8 of 8, J05 7 of 8'. The core-loop-audit summary table now says J01 9/9, B 23/23, J03 9/9, J04 8/8, J05 7/8.
- MVP-49 'remaining' lists the open emulator steps as 'J01-6, J01-7, J01-10, J03-7, J05-2 and loop B's six'. Per docs/core-loop-audit.md, J01-6, J01-7, J01-10, J03-7 and B-25 are now EVIDENCED (class E on developer APKs). Only J05-2 remains EMULATOR among the J loops.
- MVP-49 'blocked_on' lists 'EMULATOR: campaigns 2, 6, 7, 8, 9, 10 and 14'. Campaigns 7 and 15 have since run (round 8 at 2961bd9e), so the list is not reconciled.
- docs/core-loop-audit.md:227 loop B Count line says '22 evidenced ... 1 EMULATOR ... 22 of 23 = 96%'. The summary table (line 55) says B 23/23 = 100% after B-25 moved in round 8.
- docs/core-loop-audit.md:370 J01 Count line says '7 evidenced, 0 SOFTWARE, 2 EMULATOR ... 7 of 9 = 78%'. The table rows J01-6/J01-7 are EVIDENCED and the summary table says J01 9/9 = 100%.
- docs/core-loop-audit.md:14-16 opening finding still says 'no native instrumentation result is bound to this source' and 'No APK was built and no emulator was started for this audit'. This is round 4 text that the round 7/8 sections contradict, and it is not marked historical.
- docs/core-loop-audit.md:144 (flag-off table, J05) says BrowserPageQuestionInstrumentedTest 'has not been run'. Round 7 ran it: one of four methods passed on both flavors, and the rest are limited by the environment.
- The MVP-07 'remaining' list of patches/eliza references is incomplete. Beyond local-agent-voice-validation.md, standalone-paired-asr.md, calendar-reminder-contract.md, workflow-run-cancellation.md and AGENTS.md, stale patches/eliza references also exist in docs/flow-implementation-plan.md:118, docs/prototype-browser-integration.md:36 and docs/market-research/{04-redaction,13-redaction-integration,14-soc2-technical-plan,15-open-gap-technical-plan}.md. The patches/ directory does not exist. [Historical audit finding; resolved on 2026-10-12, see Implementation results.]
- docs/pilot-acceptance-runbook.md 'Release entry conditions' says to complete 'npm run android:build'. Per AGENTS.md the distribution path is 'npm run android:build:local'; plain android:build exits 2 when inputs are missing.
- The runbook 'Optional Cloud/remote agent routes' row requires 'remote pairing, revoke/expiry/restart'. PR 379 retired production Android phone pairing, and A-26 keeps the native transport admitting only https://api.eliza.app, so no Android build can reach a Cloud agent host and this row cannot be executed as written.
- MVP-01 inventory text is accurate: voice-selection.ts confirms Cloud is unconditional, and test/voice-entry-timing.test.mjs lines 7 and 9 are test.todo. The PRD AP-06 'local first' wording is still unreconciled.
- The MVP-50 claim 'no unit exists' is correct, and no emulator rehearsal record exists either (test-results/pilot-units is absent). No inventory entry says the --build debug rehearsal itself was never run.

- **P0 AP-15:a11y-click-step**: Make AccessibilityInstrumentedTest's handle activation step passable on WebView 133. One option: assert the node exposes ACTION_CLICK and is clickable, then activate through ACTION_FOCUS followed by DPAD_CENTER/ENTER. - **P0 CRITIC:notes-draft-native-admission**: Admit notes-draft:v1:device in native secureCompareExchange so Android keeps unsaved Notes edits. On Android, keepNotesDraft (agent-adapter.ts:124-135, isAndroid only) calls SecureNotesStore.saveDraft and clearDraft, which go through secureConnectionStore.compareExchange to native secureCompareExchange with slot 'notes-draft:v1:device' (SECURE_NOTES_DRAFT_SLOT). The native allowlist at AlphaConnectionPlugin.java:130 admits only inbox-drafts, inbox-operation, workflow-draft, assistant-draft, notes-records, reminder-deletions, reminder-creations, clock-handoff, notes-audio-deletions, notes-trash and cloud-delegation. So every Android save of the encrypted draft after a failed or uncertain Notes commit is rejected ('Secure draft update failed'), notesDraftState becomes 'failed', and the 'Unsaved changes are kept in an encrypted draft' path never works. Clearing a reviewed draft from Notes recovery (agent-adapter.ts:158,169,189) also fails. AlphaCredentialStore already sizes NOTES_DRAFT (32 MiB), so only the compare-exchange admission is missing. No audit flagged it: LOOP-B found only the note-origins slot. Same root cause, a different slot. *Done when:* secureCompareExchange admits exactly 'notes-draft:v1:device'. A JVM fixture or a node test that parses the Java allowlist shows saveDraft, clearDraft and the recovery reset are admitted for that slot. The LOOP-B node test that every renderer compareExchange slot is admitted natively lists notes-draft and note-origins, and it fails if either is removed. Files: `android/app/src/main/java/ai/elizaresearch/alphaphone/AlphaConnectionPlugin.java`, `apps/app/src/runtime/notes-secure-store.ts`, `apps/app/src/prototype/agent-adapter.ts`, `test/fixtures/ResidentResultTransportTest.java` Refs: MVP-25, AP-10, LOOP-B

Add a method that launches ACTION_ASSIST, focuses the full-mode composer, shows the IME, waits for WindowInsetsCompat ime() to be visible, and asserts the composer and Send fit within innerHeight. Then press Back and assert the draft persists and the activity finishes only at root. *Done when:* The new method compiles for both variants and the runner test passes. Emulator execution is tracked separately. Files: `android/app/src/androidTest/java/ai/elizaresearch/alphaphone/AssistantInstrumentedTest.java`, `android/app/src/main/java/ai/elizaresearch/alphaphone/MainActivity.java` Refs: MVP-16, MVP-22, AP-05
- **P1 AP-05:back-home-aborts-reply**: Decide and test Back/HOME behaviour during an in-flight reply. agent-adapter.ts registers backHandler and homeHandler, and both call connectionController.cancelViewNavigation(); alphaClient.cancel(). alphaClient.cancel() aborts the active foreground chat request. Collapsing the full chat to the sheet with system Back, or pressing HOME, therefore silently aborts a streaming reply, with no Stop reconciliation statement. Choose one behaviour: keep the stream alive across a chat-mode change and HOME, or treat the abort as an explicit Stop with the existing one-cancel-plus-reconciliation message. Implement it and add specs. *Done when:* New chat-continuity.spec.ts cases pass: (a) Back from full to sheet and (b) launcher-home, each fired during a fixture reply stream. Each either shows the finished reply once, or shows the Stop reconciliation text with posts===1 and no resend. npm run verify passes. Files: `apps/app/src/prototype/agent-adapter.ts`, `apps/app/src/runtime/alpha-client.ts`, `test/browser/chat-continuity.spec.ts` Refs: MVP-22, AP-05
- **P1 AP-05:home-200pct-composer-overlap**: Keep the floating composer from covering Home's last row at 200% font. At the 2961bd9e run, AccessibilityInstrumentedTest failed on standalone with 'Home at 200% font: reading order goes up from All apps to Open conversation'. At the top of the scroll, the last row of the app grid is drawn under the input-mode composer. Reserve bottom padding on the Home scroll region equal to the composer height plus the inset at the current text scale, or reorder so the composer is read last. *Done when:* A Playwright spec at 200% text scale (412x915) asserts that at scrollTop=0 no grid tile intersects the composer, and that it still holds when scrolled to the bottom. npm run verify passes. The Accessibility re-run is tracked under the emulator task. Files: `apps/app/src/prototype/template.html`, `apps/app/src/prototype/phone.css`, `apps/app/src/prototype/model.js`, `test/browser/home-compact-scroll.spec.ts` Refs: AP-05, AP-01
- **P1 AP-05:native-back-home-ime-test**: Instrumented test: draft and composer survive real system Back/HOME with the IME visible. ShellInstrumentedTest only checks that the composer sits above a shown IME on MainActivity. Extend it, or add a ChatContinuityInstrumentedTest. With the IME visible, type a draft and switch the chat between sheet and full. Press real KEYCODE_BACK through UiAutomation: chat full->sheet->rest, and the IME is dismissed. On the launcher flavor, press HOME (on standalone, background and resume). Assert the composer value and the persisted draft, that nothing was posted, and the composer rect relative to innerHeight after each step. Register the class in CLASS_REGISTRY or DEFAULT_CLASSES. *Done when:* The class compiles for both variants (compile*AndroidTestJavaWithJavac), is registered so that test/android-instrumentation-runner.test.mjs passes, and npm run verify passes. - **P1 AP-06:cloud-empty-transcript-message**: Classify an empty Cloud transcript as no-speech instead of a microphone or connection failure. cloud-voice.ts:47 throws a plain Error('Eliza Cloud returned no usable transcript.') with no code, so voiceFailure() returns null and the user sees 'Cloud voice unavailable. Check microphone access, your Cloud account and the connection'. That is misleading for silence. Give it code 'no-speech', or a Cloud-specific message, so the state is explicit. Whether 'Type transcript instead' is offered on the Cloud route is a policy choice (MVP-01); the copy must not promise a typing option that is not rendered. *Done when:* browser-speech.spec.ts 'empty Cloud transcript' asserts the honest no-speech wording, the retained clip and either the presence or absence of a typing choice consistent with the message. - **P1 J03:j03-native-grant-vs-note-ref**: Keep or clearly expire Android grants that back note source references. SelectedDocumentAccess.releaseUnused/forget releases the persisted read grant when a selection is forgotten or evicted past RECENT_LIMIT=24. It does not check AlphaMailAttachmentsPlugin's 'note-source-references' prefs, so a note's a1: link stops working with no warning. The reference map is also never pruned when notes are deleted, and once it reaches 128 entries every new link is silently fingerprint-only. Choose one approach. (a) Keep grants that a reference points to: count references, release when the last note referencing them is deleted or trashed, and add a release method the Notes delete path calls. (b) Keep releasing, but say so plainly in the summary review and in the Forget confirmation. Either way, prune unreferenced a1: entries. *Done when:* Unit/source tests (node test or Playwright with the stubbed bridge) show the chosen behavior: with (a), a linked note still opens its source after 24 more selections; with (b), the review text and the Forget confirmation state the limit. The reference prefs are pruned on note deletion. npm run verify passes. - **P2 LOOP-F:f-older-draft-version**: Handle a saved local draft that is an older version of a sent email. Open item in core-loop-status F. Keep it, as cleanup requires exact content, but label it as an earlier version of a sent email (matched by source localDraftId) and require an explicit choice before reuse. Do not auto-delete. *Done when:* After a confirmed send of an edited draft, the older saved copy is still present, is labelled as an earlier version of a sent email, and cannot be sent without a confirmation. - **P2 LOOP-F:f-refused-attachment-message**: Report a refused attachment as refused, not as 'Gmail is unavailable'. core-loop-audit J03-8 records that a refused attachment (digest mismatch, oversized, wrong type) is reported as 'Gmail is unavailable right now'. classifyGmailFailure maps every non-HTTP error to 'unavailable'. Add a distinct refusal message for the product's own validation errors. *Done when:* Every hostile case in inbox-hostile-attachment.spec.ts asserts a specific refusal message and no 'Gmail is unavailable' text. npm run verify passes. - **P2 LOOP-F:f-resumed-cleanup-reconfirm**: Re-read the provider receipt before finishing a draft cleanup resumed after reload. core-loop-status F says the saved receipt is not re-confirmed with the provider before a cleanup that resumes after a reload. When the server offers a receipt read, use it to confirm the succeeded state and provider message id before removing local copies. Files: `docs/pilot-acceptance-runbook.md` Refs: MVP-44
- **P0 RELEASE:redaction-emulator-run**: Run ResidentEgressRedaction on an emulator with the packaged resident runtime. Needs a distribution-class APK with the packaged ARM64 runtime and an owner-held Cerebras provider key fixture (MVP-34). The run is emulator-class but gated on the owner's credential. *Done when:* files/resident-redaction-complete.json with passed:true is recorded for APKs built at the current pin, and docs/verification.md is updated with run id and APK hashes. Files: `scripts/android-resident-instrumentation.mjs`, `docs/verification.md` Refs: MVP-44, MVP-34
- **P0 RELEASE:release-signer-decision**: Decide A-06, create the release key and set release-signer.json. The owner chooses managed or hardware-backed signing or a signing service, creates the key, supplies the ELIZAOS_KEYSTORE_*/VERSION_* values to the build, and records only the certificate SHA-256 and lastRelease. *Done when:* signerSha256 is set, a strict release build of both variants is signed by that certificate, and verify-apks reports signerMatches:true. Files: `android/release-signer.json`, `docs/decisions.md`, `docs/mvp-owner-actions.md` Refs: MVP-40, AP-14
- **P0 UPSTREAM:decision-a24-speech**: Owner decision A-24: is on-device speech required for a distribution build?. A strict distribution build requires qualified speech assets, and LocalSpeech functional acceptance failed on arm64 and never ran on x86_64. Without this decision, or the speech requalification under MVP-23, no artifact can pass verify-apks as distributable. *Done when:* A-24 is recorded in docs/decisions.md. Then either the speech requirement is retired in build-android/verify-apks, or MVP-23 is admitted by a human reviewer. Files: `docs/decisions.md`, `android/local-speech/qualified-runtime-manifest.json` Refs: MVP-39, MVP-23, AP-02
- **P0 UPSTREAM:strict-distribution-build**: Build all four distribution artifacts strictly and confirm the installed resident uses those bytes. Run after A-24 and the staged build: a strict build with no unpackaged or unqualified overrides, signed with the controlled release key (ELIZAOS_KEYSTORE_*), then verify-apks and build-archive. The build mechanics are software, but the artifacts are distributable only with signing keys and qualified speech. *Done when:* All four artifacts are marked distributable by verify-apks. An installed resident reports a source stamp and payload hashes equal to the built bytes. Files: `scripts/build-android.mjs`, `scripts/verify-apks.mjs`, `scripts/build-archive.mjs` Refs: MVP-39, AP-02, AP-04
- **P1 AP-01:ap01-aosp-default-home**: Default HOME in the full AOSP image. Class I: boot the selected image with Alpha as the provisioned default HOME (MVP-41). This needs owner image decisions and build infrastructure. *Done when:* A recorded AOSP boot shows Alpha as the boot-time HOME, with the drawer and assistant entry working. Files: `docs/verification.md` Refs: AP-01, MVP-41
- **P1 AP-03:ap03-owner-decisions**: Owner decisions A-02, A-16, A-20, A-26 on the managed endpoint, Cloud agents and metering. The supported service matrix depends on owner choices: is Eliza Cloud the managed endpoint, are Cloud agents allowed on Android (which would change the admittedOrigin rule), and what are the metering semantics. *Done when:* The decisions are recorded with a choice and docs/decisions.md is updated. Files: `docs/decisions.md` Refs: MVP-04, MVP-17
- **P1 AP-05:resident-reuse-arm64-campaign**: Packaged-runtime ARM64 campaign: assistant open/close keeps a live stream and enrollment. MVP-16 remaining work: run a real resident with a provider key held by the owner, on ARM64 with the packaged runtime, and open and close the assistant repeatedly during inference. *Done when:* A recorded run shows unchanged runtime identity and an intact stream and conversation across repeated invocations. Files: `docs/verification.md` Refs: MVP-16, AP-04, AP-05
- **P1 AP-06:a10-latency-decision**: Owner agrees the latency method (A-10). Close A-10: the start and end boundaries, whether review time is included, cold/warm definitions, percentile, task set and failure accounting. *Done when:* A-10 is closed in docs/decisions.md with a measurable protocol that scripts/aggregate-voice-latency.mjs can apply. Files: `docs/decisions.md`, `docs/mvp-completion-plan.md` Refs: MVP-05, A-10
- **P1 AP-07:ap07-device-acceptance**: Device acceptance: TalkBack reading of card descriptions; Doze, battery saver and lock for digests. PRD gate D: TalkBack must read the Calendar, Inbox and brief descriptions. Digest delivery must happen exactly once under Doze, battery saver, lock and power loss on the selected phone. *Done when:* A recorded physical-device run on the selected SKU at the candidate build. Files: `docs/pilot-acceptance-runbook.md` Refs: MVP-19, MVP-38, AP-15
- **P1 AP-07:ap07-real-providers**: Real Gmail grant, real inference credential and a real scheduled digest behind the Home cards. This needs MVP-35 (Google OAuth, whose code exchange currently returns 401), MVP-34 (Cerebras/Eliza Cloud credential) and the A-09 outcome. Then on an installed APK: the real Inbox card transitions, a device CalendarProvider read and a real scheduled digest delivered once to the brief card. *Done when:* Class R evidence shows loading, empty, stale and error transitions on real providers, with counts and times matching the fetched data. Files: `docs/daily-overview.md`, `docs/verification.md` Refs: MVP-19, MVP-34, MVP-35, MVP-38
- **P1 AP-09:ap09-device-acceptance**: Device acceptance with a real synced calendar and real Gmail. Calendar CRUD with recurrence scope, DST and travel zones, RSVP, read-only, revoke and lost response on a real synced account. Availability with multi-account calendars and declined invitations. Use in email with TalkBack on a real agent reply. *Done when:* D-class records exist for MVP-12, MVP-14 and MVP-26. Files: `docs/pilot-acceptance-runbook.md` Refs: MVP-12, MVP-14, MVP-26
- **P1 AP-09:ap09-owner-decisions**: Owner decisions: calendar busy defaults, attachment policy for suggestions, label scope. Confirm that cancelled and declined events are not busy and that free/busy-level calendars are offered (MVP-12). Decide whether selected Files or Photos may become attachments of a suggestion (MVP-14). Choose A-15 option (b) or (c), which decides label management. *Done when:* The decisions are recorded in docs/decisions.md. Files: `docs/decisions.md` Refs: MVP-12, MVP-14, A-15
- **P1 AP-10:device-media-notes-acceptance**: Device acceptance of camera capture, OCR on real documents, storage volumes, browser and notes lifecycle. Physical camera, real documents, low storage, gallery refresh, video interruption, English OCR quality, and Notes encryption and upgrade on the release candidate. *Done when:* The runbook records device results for MVP-25, MVP-28 and MVP-30. Files: `docs/pilot-acceptance-runbook.md` Refs: MVP-25, MVP-28, MVP-30
- **P1 AP-10:owner-alarm-decision**: Decide alarm ownership (A-11) and capture audible ring, vibration and DND evidence on a device. Clock is a reviewed handoff only; actual ringing is unverified. *Done when:* A-11 is decided and there is device evidence of ringing or an approved scope change. Files: `docs/decisions.md` Refs: MVP-27
- **P1 AP-10:owner-maps-provider**: Decide and supply the production Maps provider (A-23) as an HTTPS VITE_MAPS_BASE_URL gateway. Without it, no flag-off build can produce a route. Needs a licence and hosting decision, then R and D GPS navigation acceptance. *Done when:* A-23 is decided and a production build reads the gateway's /capabilities and produces a J04 route on a device. - **P1 AP-11:ap11-device-acceptance**: Device acceptance of workflow runs, approvals, cancellation and history with real receipts. On selected hardware with the resident runtime: one multistep workflow, approval by notification, cancel at a step boundary, worker death, reconnect showing exact history, no duplicate external effect, and no invented receipt. Doze, lock and battery saver for notices. *Done when:* A D-class record for each scenario, with exact receipts, run ids and source identity. Files: `docs/pilot-acceptance-runbook.md` Refs: MVP-32, MVP-47, MVP-49, AP-11
- **P1 AP-11:ap11-real-inference-quality**: Measure generated-candidate quality with real hosted Qwen inference. This needs the pilot Cerebras key (A-20) or Eliza Cloud credits (A-02). Exercise generation of bounded candidates, refusal of unsupported capabilities and Use/Save/Run separation with real model output. *Done when:* A recorded R-class run of candidate generation against the hosted model, with outcomes per prompt category. - **P1 AP-11:ap11-trigger-scope-decision**: Owner decision on narrowing workflows to manual triggers. PRD F15 and its acceptance require enable/disable triggers, schedules, duplicate triggers, overlapping runs and a revoked source. The product ships manual-trigger typed workflows only; digests are the only scheduled kind. No entry in docs/decisions.md records this narrowing. The owner should approve it, or require typed workflow triggers. *Done when:* A decision entry in docs/decisions.md records the trigger scope, and the PRD F15 acceptance text matches it. Files: `docs/decisions.md`, `docs/prd.md`, `docs/flow-audit-and-prd.md` Refs: MVP-32, AP-11
- **P1 AP-12:decision-a06-update**: Owner decision A-06: update authority before any update check is shown. Files: `docs/decisions.md` Refs: AP-12, AP-14, MVP-33
- **P1 AP-12:device-settings-acceptance**: Device and image acceptance of every Settings handoff and readback. - **P1 AP-12:real-accounts-connections**: Real Eliza Cloud account and Gmail grant for the Accounts and Connections pages. - **P1 CRITIC:mvp51-handoff-complete**: Complete and validate the operational handoff with an independent operator after the pilot. This depends on MVP-50. - **P1 J01:j01-real-provider-account**: Save to a real synced calendar account (Google CalendarProvider) chosen in the destination picker. Needs the strict or packaged build, a real Eliza Cloud account or credits for the hosted Qwen on Cerebras, and an ARM64 target. It also exercises the plugin-openai usage-before-rejection change consumed at this pin. *Done when:* Recorded R/E evidence that runtime identity is unchanged, owned work is cancelled correctly and the conversation is kept across repeated assistant opens and closes, with real inference. Files: `scripts/android-resident-instrumentation.mjs` Refs: MVP-16, MVP-08, AP-04
- **P2 AP-01:ap01-a22-decision**: Owner decision A-22 on landscape and rotation. Whether the responsive landscape layout ships (a), activities are locked to portrait (b), or rotation is allowed without a landscape layout (c). *Done when:* A-22 is closed with the chosen option, and app.config.json orientation matches it. Files: `docs/decisions.md` Refs: AP-01, MVP-06
- **P2 AP-01:ap01-upstream-launcher-apps**: Upstream a component- and profile-aware SystemLauncherApps. MVP-53 marks this as NEEDS upstream so that the product-local LauncherLibrary.java can be replaced. It requires an upstream elizaOS merge by others; vendor/eliza must not be edited directly. *Done when:* The upstream commit is merged and pinned, and LauncherLibrary.java is replaced by the shared module with tests unchanged. Files: `android/app/src/main/java/ai/elizaresearch/alphaphone/LauncherLibrary.java` Refs: AP-01, MVP-53, MVP-08
- **P2 AP-02:upstream-launcher-apps**: Upstream a component- and profile-aware SystemLauncherApps. MVP-53 marks this NEEDS upstream, so that LauncherLibrary.java can be replaced by the shared module. It needs a reviewed upstream merge followed by a pin move. *Done when:* The upstream change is merged, the pin moves, and LauncherLibrary.java is removed or reduced to configuration. Files: `android/app/src/main/java/ai/elizaresearch/alphaphone/LauncherLibrary.java` Refs: MVP-53, MVP-08
- **P2 AP-04:upstream-history-client-id**: Upstream: expose clientMessageId in history rows, plus a Cloud-agent cancel route and history cursor. The read after Stop matches by text and time because upstream history rows lack the client message ID. Cloud agents have no turn-abort route or older-page cursor. Cloud-hosted agents are optional scope. *Done when:* An upstream PR is merged and pinned, and scheduleStoppedReply matches by clientMessageId. Files: `vendor/eliza (upstream PR, not a local edit)`, `apps/app/src/runtime/connection-ui.tsx` Refs: MVP-08, AP-04
- **P2 AP-05:rotation-decision**: Owner decision A-22: does landscape ship. This decision sets whether resize continuity must hold across rotation on the target device. *Done when:* A-22 is recorded as decided. Files: `docs/decisions.md` Refs: AP-05, AP-15
- **P2 AP-09:ap09-upstream-calendar-read**: Upstream: an availability-carrying selected-calendar read in plugin-native-calendar. This needs a reviewed upstream commit, with a journal result bound that fits the 200-interval contract, so that the Alpha-side reader can be removed. *Done when:* The upstream commit is pinned and CalendarAvailabilityReader is removed. Files: `vendor/eliza/plugins/plugin-native-calendar`, `android/app/src/main/java/ai/elizaresearch/alphaphone/CalendarAvailabilityReader.java` Refs: MVP-12
- **P2 AP-10:upstream-credential-slots**: Upstream JsonCredentialSlots and AlphaCredentialStore.requireCapacity allowance for lowered Trash limits. Until upstream accepts existing slots above a lowered limit, notes-trash:v1:device must not drop below 32 MiB. *Done when:* A reviewed upstream commit is pinned and the policy test exercises a lowered native limit. Files: `patches/eliza`, `upstream.lock.json` Refs: MVP-15 [Historical audit finding; resolved on 2026-10-12, see Implementation results.]
- **P2 AP-12:decision-a19-erase**: Owner decision A-19: backup and erase policy for a Settings erase control. F18 lists 'delete exact selected data'. Settings has no erase control because A-19 is undecided. *Done when:* A-19 is decided. If option (ii) is chosen, a follow-up software task exists for the confirmed in-app erase. Files: `docs/decisions.md` Refs: AP-12, MVP-33
- **P2 AP-12:decision-voice-policy**: Voice policy (MVP-01) before voice preferences in Settings are final. The Voice section currently shows only Cloud speech, 'Wake word: Not available' and agent-managed personality. Any user-facing voice preference depends on the voice policy decision. *Done when:* The voice policy is recorded, and the Settings voice rows match it. Files: `docs/decisions.md` Refs: MVP-01, AP-12
- **P2 AP-14:ap14-denton-reference**: Optional: record Denton licence document reference. The owner can supply a reference (not the document) for the reference field. Nothing is blocked without it. *Done when:* The reference field is populated, or the owner declines and that is recorded. Files: `licenses/font-licenses.json` Refs: MVP-43, A-21
- **P2 J03:j03-device-acceptance**: J03 end to end on the final AOSP image and a physical phone. There is no I or D evidence: image boot with the HOME role, real provider files and PDFs, and user acceptance of the review and summary flow. *Done when:* I and D records exist for the full loop at the release source. Files: `docs/verification.md` Refs: J03, MVP-30, MVP-49
- **P2 LOOP-B:b-ci-reminder-tap**: Dispatch resident-android recovery-ui for ReminderTapProcessDeath. The exact reminder tap after process death is covered only by hosted CI job recovery-ui, which has never been dispatched under the CI cost policy. Owner approval is needed. *Done when:* A passing recovery-ui run at the current source is recorded. Files: `.github/workflows/resident-android.yml`, `docs/ci-cost-policy.md` Refs: F-16, MVP-27
- **P2 LOOP-B:b-reminder-detail-delete-review**: Decide whether reminder deletion from the detail page gets a review step (A-25). Reminder deletion from the detail page is immediate ('Reminder cancelled'). Events have no Trash or Undo. An owner decision (A-25) is needed before implementing a review, Undo or Trash. *Done when:* A-25 is decided and recorded; any follow-up implementation is filed as software. Files: `docs/decisions.md`, `apps/app/src/prototype/reminder-adapter.ts` Refs: A-25, AP-15, MVP-48
- **P2 LOOP-B:b-upstream-event-identity**: Upstream: stable per-event identity or extended property in plugin-native-calendar. CalendarProvider row ids are reused, so Android events cannot link back to their note. This needs an upstream change returning a stable identity from list, then a pin bump. *Done when:* The upstream pin exposes a stable event identity and noteOriginSupported('event') can return true on native. Files: `vendor/eliza`, `upstream.lock.json` Refs: B-28, A-25
- **P2 LOOP-D:d-upstream-dossier**: Upstream: register POST /api/workflow/hosted/dossier in plugin-workflow plugin-routes.ts. Applies if d-dossier-route confirms the route is not registered. Fix through a reviewed upstream commit and a pin bump, never by editing vendor/eliza. *Done when:* The pinned upstream registers the route, and an upstream test dispatches through the plugin route table. Files: `vendor/eliza/plugins/plugin-workflow/src/plugin-routes.ts` Refs: MVP-38
- **P2 LOOP-E:E-passkeys-decision**: Decide A-12 (passkeys) and A-13 (third-party cookies). Passkeys are not implemented (E-19), and F09 acceptance names a passkey test site. Third-party cookies are blocked (setAcceptThirdPartyCookies(false)), which may break embedded sign-ins under P-04. *Done when:* decisions.md records both choices and the F09 acceptance text matches them. Files: `docs/decisions.md` Refs: A-12, A-13, E-19
- **P2 LOOP-F:f-upstream-mailbox-revision**: Add a mailbox revision to the upstream search contract. The F-12 weak point: without a revision, a message removed between pages can silently skip a row. This needs a reviewed upstream change, not a vendor edit. *Done when:* Upstream search returns a revision and the renderer detects a mismatch between pages, with a test. Files: `vendor/eliza/packages/cloud/shared/src/lib/services/agent-google-connector/inbox-provider.ts` Refs: F-12, MVP-09
- **P2 UPSTREAM:decision-a02-remote-pairing**: Owner decision A-02, then a real private remote host for browser transport qualification. MVP-17 cannot record the supported browser development scope until A-02 says whether remote pairing stays. Real pairing, account return, revoke and Automations behaviour need a real host. *Done when:* A-02 is decided. Then either the remote route is documented as supported and qualified against a real host, or it is removed or retired from the flag-off browser. Files: `docs/decisions.md`, `docs/browser-dev-parity.md`, `test/browser/connection-boundaries.production.spec.ts` Refs: MVP-17, AP-03
- **P2 UPSTREAM:device-acceptance-runtime**: Device acceptance of the resident runtime and of upstream-consumed modules on real hardware. No D-class evidence exists at any pin for resident reuse, notice delivery, media or journal. *Done when:* Device runs recorded per the pilot runbook for the distributable build. Files: `docs/pilot-acceptance-runbook.md` Refs: MVP-16, MVP-39, MVP-10, MVP-11
- **P2 UPSTREAM:upstream-calendar-gmail-changes**: Submit two upstream changes: Calendar review names the account; Gmail search pages carry a mailbox revision. Identified by the core-loop audit. Neither blocks a step. Needs an upstream PR merged by the maintainers, then a pin move. *Done when:* Both changes are merged upstream and consumed at a new reviewed pin, with no local patch. Files: `vendor/eliza (read-only; submit upstream)` Refs: MVP-09

## External blockers (summary)

- Owner decisions: MVP-01 voice send and route policy (P-01/P-07, A-04, A-24; WP5 ships a policy constant that defaults to auto-send); A-10 latency method; A-09 powered-off execution; A-06 signer, update and rollback authority plus the release key; A-01 Pixel 10 SKU and device inputs; A-02/A-16/A-20/A-26 services, metering and endpoints; A-05 new-mail notifications; A-11 alarm ownership; A-12/A-13/A-14 passkeys, cookies and browser vault fill; A-19 backup and erase; A-22 landscape; A-23 Maps provider and HTTPS gateway; A-25 deletion recoverability; workflow trigger scope; MVP-52 always-on listening and hardware-key invocation (P0 scope gate, undecided); AGENTS.md wording change.
- Coordination: the main checkout's vendor/eliza is at 3cca1ee4, not the locked 13c98575. Reset it with git submodule update only after coordinating with the concurrent Codex process. Worktrees must init their own submodule.
- Distribution builds: no distributable B build exists at the pin. It needs the packaged resident runtime (WP8 attempts the build), the speech qualification or an A-24 waiver, a signed release key (A-06), assetlinks.json on an owned domain, and the Main required-checks ruleset installed and proven to block merges.
- Emulator campaigns, no human needed:
- LauncherLibrary, Assistant, Flow, AssistantResidentReuse, Rotation, Accessibility (after the 200% fix), work-profile drawer, renderer-recovery and the cross-flavor upgrade.
- The microphone campaigns and test-mocks voice classes at the pin.
- Every new class from WP2 (connection guards, IME, vault, note-origin link, J01/J04/J05 journeys, a1: references, SelectedDocument, Use in email, availability WebView, workflow notice phases, crash log, theme restart).
- RealClock on API 35, ClockAgentReview and ReminderTapProcessDeath in the recovery-ui job, test-installed-upgrade, the HostedProcessRestart/HostedBackgroundWorker test-mocks pair, digest native sources, BrowserFlow live, the CI-2 WebView 157 BrowserReading/BrowserPageQuestion job, the CI-3 default campaign, resident-android.yml native and recovery-ui dispatches, provision-unit and pilot-update rehearsals, and the SAF folder and notification-listener runs.
- Real integrations (R): live Eliza Cloud sign-in, credits, expiry, self-revocation and wrong owner; real Qwen/Cerebras inference with ResidentService and ResidentEgressRedaction on an arm64 packaged runtime; Google OAuth code exchange 401 fix plus deployed inbox-v1 routes and a granted test account; one owner-authorized real send, reply and forward; real Cloud transcription and synthesis; real agent proposals (clock, notes, documents, web pages); a real synced Google calendar; a Proton Pass test vault; Maps against the production gateway.
- Upstream changes by others: component- and profile-aware SystemLauncherApps; clientMessageId in history plus a Cloud-agent cancel route and history cursor; register POST /api/workflow/hosted/dossier in plugin-routes.ts; a stable event identity in plugin-native-calendar; an availability-carrying calendar read; Calendar review naming the account; a mailbox revision in Gmail search; JsonCredentialSlots capacity allowance; include_granted_scopes for Gmail if needed.
- AOSP image (I): build and boot Cuttlefish with the staged signed launcher as default HOME and DeskClock present; admit the Pixel 10 lock; signed update and recovery drill.
- Device and user acceptance (D): physical HOME role and emergency access, work profile, TalkBack, microphone, speaker and Bluetooth latency samples, camera and OCR on real posters, Doze, battery saver, lock and notification delivery, alarm ring, vibration and DND, GPS navigation, four-unit pilot with soak and signed update, and the independent-operator handoff validation (MVP-51).

## Critic notes

I checked the inventory (MVP-01 to MVP-53), the requirements.json AP rows and flow-audit F01-F19 against the 26 audits. Missed items: MVP-51 (operational handoff) and MVP-52 (AOSP listening, hardware invocation) had no task at all. Two MVP-13 remainders were also missing: own-notice questions on Android, and folder or notification-listener emulator runs. AP-08 and AP-13 are deferred and the code agrees: mvp-features.ts disables phone, messages, contacts and wallet outside the development profile. The F01 HOME-role request exists in settings-adapter.ts:401-403. New code finding: AlphaConnectionPlugin.java:130 secureCompareExchange omits 'notes-draft:v1:device', which apps/app/src/runtime/notes-secure-store.ts and apps/app/src/prototype/agent-adapter.ts:131,158,169,189 use on Android. The LOOP-B node test that every renderer compareExchange slot is admitted natively should cover both missing slots (note-origins and notes-draft).

Spot-checks of audit claims:
- Confirmed (11):
  - note-origins:v1:device is absent from the native allowlist.
  - test/scan-event.test.mjs was deleted in 8b88486c.
  - test/voice-entry-timing.test.mjs still has test.todo.
  - scripts/start-local-voice-agent.mjs uses ALPHA_ELIZA_SOURCE with a default path of ~/eliza-workspace/milady/eliza.
  - The patches/ directory does not exist, so the AGENTS.md references to patches/eliza are stale. [Historical audit finding; resolved on 2026-10-12, see Implementation results.]
  - PENDING_AVATAR_INITIALS MC/JP/+2 has zero occurrences in template.html, so the allowance is stale.
  - The vendor/eliza checkout HEAD is 3cca1ee4 while git tree and upstream.lock pin 13c98575.
  - plugin-workflow plugin-routes.ts has no dossier route.
  - maps-adapter.ts has no openExternal or geo handoff.
  - Clock repeat days are gated on the EXTRA_DAYS capability.
  - The 10.0.2.2:47850 Maps gateway exists only behind testMocksEnabled.
- Plausible, not checked further: Find in page is native-only in the browser adapter.

Caution on the UPSTREAM P0 'reset vendor/eliza': memory says Codex runs concurrently in this checkout, so the reset should be coordinated rather than run blindly. It may be better classed as an operator step.

## Implementation results

Recorded on 2026-10-12 after the ten work packages ran. The nine code packages merged into
`claude/mvp-completion` in the order WP8, WP9, WP1, WP2, WP4, WP5, WP3, WP6, WP7, with two
integration fixes (`cc97c575`, `bfcd47e2`), and this ledger package (WP10) ran last on the result.
Nothing was pushed. The upstream pin stayed `13c9857598`.

Evidence class of the whole integration: **S** (source, host JVM and browser tests) and a
**B compile** (Gradle compiled the app and instrumentation Java of both debug flavors; no APK was
assembled). No emulator, AOSP image, real integration or device was used, so no task below moved
anything to class E, I, R or D. The details are in
[verification](verification.md#mvp-completion-integration-2026-10-12-source-browser-and-compile-checks-only).
`npm run verify` did not pass end to end on the integration machine, because `onnxruntime-web` was
missing from the shared `node_modules`; each later step passed separately with a lockfile-identical
`node_modules`. Neither APK script got past its input check (no speech AAR matching the committed
digest).

Task identifiers below are those of [All remaining tasks](#all-remaining-tasks).

### What the integration changed in the counts

The core loop audit was recomputed from its step tables: 131 of 148 closable steps evidenced
(89%), with 12 EMULATOR, 5 CI, 24 HUMAN, 13 DEVICE and 1 UPSTREAM. The closable total grew because
rows were added (six browser classes in loop E, two phone-source and dossier steps in D, one real
agent answer in J03), and A-29 and E-14 moved back to EMULATOR. The only open EMULATOR step of the
pilot journeys is J05-2. No requirement changed status; every percentage in the tables at the top
of this audit was estimated before the packages ran and was not re-estimated.
